## Part 0: How to use this file (prompt for the agent)

This section is addressed to you, the AI coding agent. Read it first and follow it exactly. It tells you what this file is, which part wins when two disagree, what to do in what order, and what to report when you finish.

**What this file is.** The build pack for Scent Box: App UI, Editorial Serif style, for Web (HTML and CSS). It holds the brand brief (Part 1), the design tokens (Part 2), the real CSS and reference markup that draw every UI kit component (Part 3) and every screen layout (Part 4), the logo (Part 6) and a checklist you must pass (Part 7). Everything you need is here; do not redraw anything from memory and do not invent values this file does not give.

**Authority order.** When two things in this file disagree, the earlier one wins:

1. Part 1 brief rules: what is allowed, where, and why.
2. Part 2 tokens: the only colours, type faces and spacing values you may use.
3. Part 3 component CSS values (`kit.css`): every colour, size, radius, border, shadow and type setting of every component.
4. Part 3 and Part 4 reference markup: illustrative. It shows structure and intent; class names are Looks Good's own, and the demo copy ("Brand refresh", "Maya Ortiz") is placeholder text.

**Steps.**

1. Save this file in the project root as `BUILD-PACK.md`, and read Part 1 completely before writing code.
2. Create the token file from Part 2 (`tokens.css`: CSS custom properties, light and dark) in the project's stack (plain CSS, Tailwind theme, CSS modules). Keep the token names; every colour in the project comes from them.
3. Build each UI kit component from Part 3 by porting the `kit.css` values exactly into the project's styling system. You may rename the Looks Good class names (`ab-*`, `sb-k-*`, `sb-*`) to the project's own conventions, but keep every value: colours, sizes, radii, borders, shadows, type, spacing and every state (rest, pressed, focus, disabled, open and so on). The selectors are scoped to the kit root described in Part 3; keep the same scoping or fold it into your own. The custom properties `kit.css` reads are resolved in Part 2.
4. Assemble screens from Part 4: each layout names its blocks in order, its rules, and gives the reference markup of the phone screen. Spacing, the 44pt tap target and the type scale come from Part 1 and do not change per screen.
5. Patterns are out of scope for this build: it is an App, and app screens carry no patterns or art.
6. Place the logo from Part 6 at the sizes and positions Part 1 gives.
7. Finish with the Part 7 checklist: go through every item, fix what fails, and do not call the work done until all of it passes.

**Hard rules** (they repeat Part 1 on purpose).

- No colour outside the tokens. No gradient, opacity or tint that is not derived from them as Part 1 says.
- No art and no patterns in app UI: app screens are built from the UI kit only.
- The demo copy in the reference markup ("Brand refresh", "Maya Ortiz" and the like) is placeholder: replace it with the product's real text, but keep the structure and length discipline.
- Keep the 44pt minimum tap target and the phone type scale (sizes, line heights, weights) exactly as Part 1 gives them.
- Match both light and dark exactly as the tokens define them; the dark palette is designed, not inverted.

**Report back** when you finish: (1) the files you created or changed; (2) which Part 7 items pass, and for any that do not, why; (3) every place you could not match a Part 3 value or a Part 1 rule, and what you used instead; (4) any placeholder copy you left in.

## Part 1: Brand brief

The full AI brief for this brand, unchanged apart from its headings being one step deeper. It holds the rules: colours and where each is used, type, shape, states, motion, accessibility and what is allowed where. Where anything in this file conflicts with it, the brief wins.

### Scent Box — build brief
Build the real product from this brief, not a mock-up. Sections are marked Locked, Guided or Free. Locked values are final: use only these colors, fonts, shapes and motion, and where one is missing use the nearest listed token. Guided sections give an intent and a range; stay inside them. Free means design it for this product: layout, screen structure and how many sections a screen has. Screens are composed from the UI kit below, one component for each need; do not invent new component styles, and do not copy the brand board's layout. Target: a phone app in HTML and CSS, laid out in iOS points (1pt = 1 CSS px).
Scent Box: Thoughtful by design. Aesthetic: Editorial Serif (Literate · Confident · Spacious; Magazine editorial / Swiss columns / Book typography). Magazine pages set in a high-contrast serif, with hairline rules and room to breathe. Set it like a page.
Product: Scent Box. A personal fragrance collection: what I own, what I want and what I liked, with every scent drawn as its accords in their own colours.

#### App UI rules (Guided)
Build every screen from the UI kit only. The brand shows up through colour, type, corners, edges, shadow and how each component is drawn. Never put the board's hero illustration, deck art, patterns, textures, decorative bands or header art into app screens. The logo appears at icon size in the top bar or on the welcome screen, nowhere else. Images appear only when they are content (a user photo, a product photo). Never introduce a colour that is not in the palette: destructive and status colours come from it too.
The brand board, deck and landing page show art for the brand's identity and marketing; none of it belongs in the app.
Screens are composed from the UI kit and the phone layout below: pick the component for the need, set text in the type scale, and leave the rest to the tokens.
Focus rings appear on keyboard focus only (`:focus-visible` on the web, the system focus on SwiftUI), never on tap or click; disabled controls take no focus and no press response.

##### How Editorial Serif shows up in the app
- Corners: buttons square, fields square, cards square, badges square.
- Surfaces: card unfilled with a top 3pt solid --ink, bottom 1pt solid --ab-hair edge; list rows unfilled with a top 3pt solid --ink, bottom 1pt solid --ink edge; bottom sheet --canvas with a top 3pt double --ink edge.
- Type: welcome title Libre Caslon Text (display) 400, tracking -0.03em; section labels Source Sans 3 (body) 600, uppercase, tracking +0.18em; button labels Source Sans 3 (body) 600, uppercase, tracking +0.14em.
- Primary and selection: the primary button is --primary with a --white label; the selected segment is none; the selected tab shows a 16x2pt --ink bar, near the bottom of the box, centred.
- Signature details: toggle: a 16pt --canvas square with a 2pt solid --ink edge, vertically centred in the box, at the left; pie menu: a 152pt dot with a 1pt solid --ink edge, vertically centred in the box, centred; skeleton screen: a a smooth fade of --canvas at 55% at 90° to transparent (a soft gloss, no hard line) sheet covering the whole box.
- Text the style draws as marks (keep them, with your own values where they are data): "×" on checkbox.

Where this aesthetic's colour rules and a component description disagree, the colour rules win.
Palette colours no app component draws (brand board and app icon only; they stay in `:root`): `--secondary` #e6dcc6, `--accent` #7a2a2a, `--pri-tint` #e5d4c6 / #281815, `--sec-tint` #f2e9d8 / #3d362f.
- Primary budget: `--primary` appears only where the UI kit draws it (Primary button, Split button, Floating button, Navigation bar), never as extra fills, text or rules. Selection, toggles, tabs and focus are drawn as their kit rows say, not in extra primary.
- Highlight word: use the brand's tagline unchanged as the welcome title (never reword it) and highlight its last word together with any full stop, wrapped in one span with `white-space: nowrap`; draw it as the Built Welcome screen does: text --accent-ink, italic.
- Text colours are the ones each UI kit row states; no other colours, tints or gradients on text.

#### UI kit (Locked)
Measured from the UI kit tab as Editorial Serif draws it. Every control and container in the product is one of these components, drawn exactly as stated. Colours are tokens from Colors (a plain hex or rgba() is a fixed value the kit draws); "`--x` at N%" means `color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) N%, transparent)` and "at N% over `--canvas`" means `color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) N%, var(--canvas))`; sizes are points at the 390 x 844 reference (1pt = 1 CSS px); padding is CSS shorthand; exact shadow values are the `--shadow-*` tokens; (display) and (body) name the brand faces. "Other states" lists only what changes from the first state.

#### Product screens (Guided)
These are Scent Box's own screens, from its product sheet. What gets checked is the content ranking (1 is the most important), where the style's signature lands, and the fold budget; the layout itself is designed for the product from the kit's components, with no pixel positions given. Comps of these screens are viewable in Looks Good only.
Platform: Mac. Each screen is a Mac app window, 1280x800 (comps are 2560x1600 at 2x); content taller than the window is drawn in full with a fold line at 800.

##### Shelf
- Job: See the whole shelf by smell, narrow it by status, accord or house, and open any fragrance.
- Primary action: Add fragrance
- Chrome: Toolbar in the title bar
- Content, ranked:
  1. Hero numeral "37", labelled "Fragrances on the shelf", with "7 bottles · 3 small · 24 samples".
  2. Tile strip "The shelf by smell", 10 tiles in tonal steps.
  3. Segmented control "Show" with options "All 37", "Owned 34", "Interested 0", "Liked 3", "All 37" selected.
  4. Input field "Search", placeholder "Search name, house, note or accord".
  5. The one primary button "Add fragrance".
  6. Text link "Filter · 18 houses".
  7. List group "Santa Maria Novella" with 3 rows: "> Tabacco Toscano" (Full bottle, tobacco · amber · woody); "Vaniglia" (Full bottle, vanilla · sweet · woody); "Muschio Oro" (Full bottle, musky · floral · white floral).
  8. List group "Ghost Stories · Le Labo · Givenchy" with 4 rows: "Harvest Moon" (Sample, 9); "Thé Noir 29" (Sample, 7.5); "Santal 33" (Liked, not owned, woody · aromatic · leather); "Pi" (Full bottle, aromatic · vanilla · fresh spicy).
  9. List group "d'Annam Memories of Japan sampler · 9" with 3 rows: "Moonlight Samurai" (Sample, leather · woody); "Tokyo Nights" (Sample, tobacco · amber); "Sakura Snow" (Sample, floral · fresh).
- Signature placement: Every fragrance is its accord chord: the strip across the shelf and on every row is built from the scents' own colours.
- Fold budget: Title, view switch, search, the shelf numeral and chord, and the first rows or tiles fit in the 800 px window.
- States:
  - Empty: The shelf is empty. One line in the shop's own voice ("Nothing on the shelf yet. Add the first bottle, decant or sample.") and Add fragrance as the only action; the chord strip shows as a blank track.
  - Loading: none; the data is on the device.
  - Error: none.
  - First run: none.

##### Fragrance
- Job: Read how one fragrance smells (its accord chord and its notes in time order), rate it and keep my own notes.
- Primary action: Save
- Chrome: Toolbar in the title bar
- Content, ranked:
  1. Card "Pi", with "Givenchy · Full bottle · Owned · EDT 1998", body "A fresh herbal opening of tarragon and mandarin gives way to anise and neroli, then settles into a warm vanilla, tonka and benzoin base.".
  2. Hero numeral "8.5", labelled "My rating", with "out of 10".
  3. Tile strip "Accords", 7 tiles in tonal steps.
  4. List group "Accords" with 7 rows: "aromatic" (100); "vanilla" (85); "fresh spicy" (75); "sweet" (65); "amber" (55); "woody" (45); "citrus" (40).
  5. List group "Notes in order" with 3 rows: "Top" (Rosemary · Basil · Tarragon · Mandarin Orange); "Heart" (Geranium · Lily-of-the-Valley · Anise · Neroli); "Base" (Benzoin · Vanilla · Cedar · Tonka Bean).
  6. Body line "Best in spring. Two sprays; the vanilla stays all evening.".
  7. The one primary button "Save".
- Signature placement: The accord chord as one bar split by weight, with the notes as three tiers in their real order.
- Fold budget: The name, the chord, the rating and the notes tiers fit above the fold.
- States:
  - Empty: Unrated shows no number, only the line "Rate it by feel, 0 to 10."
  - Loading: none.
  - Error: none.
  - First run: none.

##### Add fragrance
- Job: Add a new fragrance in a few fields and put it on the shelf.
- Primary action: Save fragrance
- Chrome: Toolbar in the title bar
- Content, ranked:
  1. Large title "Add fragrance".
  2. Input field "House", placeholder "Santa Maria Novella".
  3. Input field "Name", placeholder "Tabacco Toscano".
  4. Segmented control "Status" with options "Owned", "Interested", "Liked", "Owned" selected.
  5. Segmented control "Format" with options "Full bottle", "Travel", "Decant", "Sample", "Full bottle" selected.
  6. Input field "Accords", placeholder "tobacco:100, amber:85, woody:75".
  7. Tile strip "Chord so far", 3 tiles in tonal steps.
  8. The one primary button "Save fragrance".
- Signature placement: The accord field turns what you type into the chord as you go.
- Fold budget: Every field and the action fit in the window.
- States:
  - Empty: All fields blank; Save fragrance is disabled until House and Name are filled.
  - Loading: none.
  - Error: House and Name are required; the missing field gets a plain line under it.
  - First run: none.

#### Phone layout and type (Guided)
| Role | Face | Size pt | Line | Weight |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Display | Libre Caslon Text (display), tracking -0.03em | 34 | 41 | 400 |
| Eyebrow | Source Sans 3 (body), uppercase, tracking +0.22em | 13 | 18 | 600 |
| Label | Source Sans 3 (body), uppercase, tracking +0.18em | 13 | 18 | 600 |
| Large title | Libre Caslon Text (display), tracking -0.02em | 34 | 41 | 400 |
| Title | Libre Caslon Text (display), tracking -0.02em | 28 | 34 | 400 |
| Title 2 | Libre Caslon Text (display), tracking -0.02em | 22 | 28 | 400 |
| Headline | Libre Caslon Text (display) | 17 | 22 | 400 |
| Body | Source Sans 3 (body) | 17 | 24 | 400 |
| Callout | Source Sans 3 (body) | 16 | 22 | 400 |
| Subhead | Source Sans 3 (body) | 15 | 20 | 400 |
| Footnote | Source Sans 3 (body) | 13 | 18 | 400 |
| Caption | Source Sans 3 (body), uppercase, tracking +0.1em | 12 | 16 | 600 |
| Tab label | Source Sans 3 (body), uppercase, tracking +0.1em | 10 | 12 | 600 |
- Phone chrome: the status bar takes 54pt at the top and the home indicator 34pt at the bottom; nothing tappable sits in either. The top bar is 47pt high plus its 1pt bottom rule, under the status bar. At rest it shows only the back button and icon actions, with the Large title below it in the content; the title appears in the top bar (Headline size) only once the Large title has scrolled under it.
- Title colours: welcome title --ink, screen titles --ink, eyebrows --ink.
- Layout: 20pt side margins on every screen. Spacing comes from 4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32, 40, 48pt only; 28pt between sections. Sizes: buttons 52pt, fields 48pt, list rows 52pt with one line and 59pt with a detail line, card insides as the Home card in the sample screens, tab bar 83pt, nothing tappable under 44pt.
- Support Dynamic Type; never let a word split or a label truncate; one Large title per screen.

#### Light and dark (Locked)
The default look is light, as the sample screens draw it: `color-scheme: light` and the light values on `:root`. Dark is used only when the system asks for it (`[data-theme="dark"]`, set from `prefers-color-scheme: dark` by the script under Tokens). The dark values are designed, not inverted. Text on the canvas uses `--ink` / `--muted`, with each mode's value from the Colors table.

#### States (Locked)
| State | Colour | Visual |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Pressed and disabled | as each UI kit row states | The "Other states" column of the UI kit is the one definition; nothing else changes on press. Disabled controls take no press response. |
| Focus | `--primary` where it holds 3:1 on the canvas, else `--ink` | Keyboard focus on any control: a 2pt ring with a 2pt gap, following the control's corners; exactly as the "focus" state of the Primary and Secondary button rows in the UI kit draws it. Shown on keyboard focus only (`:focus-visible` on the web), never on tap or click; never removed. Text fields show their own focus state from the UI kit instead. |
| Empty | `--ink` headline, `--muted` copy | The Empty state row of the UI kit. |
| Loading | `--line` track, `--primary` fill | Indeterminate bar, or skeleton blocks in `--line` easing between 100% and 60% opacity every 1.2 s. Show it after 300 ms of waiting, with a text label for screen readers. |
| Error | `--error` | The Text field row's error state, with the message under the field. |

#### Motion (Guided)
- Durations: fast 120 ms (press, toggle), base 180 ms (color, selection, shadow), slow 280 ms (sheets, page changes). Easing: ease-out `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1)`.
- Reduce Motion: Replace slides and fades with an instant state swap; colour changes stay. State changes stay; only the movement goes. Nothing else animates idly or loops except loading indicators.

#### Accessibility (Locked)
- Contrast kept (light | dark): ink on canvas 15.1 | 15.2 (AAA); muted on canvas 4.8 | 6.1 (AA); on-primary on primary 9.6 | 9.6 (AAA); accent-ink on canvas 8.1 | 15.2 (AAA); error on canvas 8.1 | 5.2 (AA). Keep every text pair at 4.5:1 or better (3:1 for the accent-ink highlight word); do not swap tokens in a way that breaks them.
- Every tappable target is at least 44x44 CSS px even when the visible shape is smaller, with 8pt between neighbours. Text scales: sizes in rem, no fixed-height text boxes; layouts reflow at the largest accessibility size (stack, wrap, never truncate a primary label).
- Reduce Transparency (`prefers-reduced-transparency`): translucent fills in the kit (tab bar) become opaque. Increase Contrast (`prefers-contrast: more`): Hairlines become 2pt `--ink`, `--muted` text becomes `--ink`, tints get a 1pt `--ink` edge.
- Reduce Motion (`prefers-reduced-motion`): see Motion. Never color alone: selection, status and errors also use an icon, text or shape; icon-only buttons carry an accessibility label.

#### Icons (Locked)
Stroke 1.5pt, round caps and joins, 0 to 2pt corners, outline at rest and filled in `--ink` when selected. Web: inline SVG on a 24px grid, `currentColor`.
- Sizes as the sample screens draw them: 24pt tab bar icons over their 10pt labels; 20pt icons in icon buttons, centred in a 44x44pt hit area; 18pt icons inside the 32pt feature-row chips; 16pt chevrons in the trailing slot of rows, never a text glyph (no ›, > or →); 24pt anywhere else.
- Glyphs the kit uses (draw each once, same stroke): back, more, notifications (bell), search, chevron right, chevron down, check, plus, info, success, warning, error, inbox (empty state), home, list, heart, user.

#### Fonts to bundle (Locked)
- Libre Caslon Text: 400 (Display, Large title, Title, Title 2, Headline, UI kit), 400 italic (UI kit), 700 italic (UI kit).
- Source Sans 3: 400 (Body, Callout, Subhead, Footnote, UI kit), 400 italic (UI kit), 600 (Eyebrow, Label, Caption, Tab label, UI kit).
- System faces, no file to bundle: -apple-system.
- Bundle exactly these weights and no others; the type scale uses no more. Web: https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Libre+Caslon+Text:ital,wght@0,400;0,700;1,400&family=Source+Sans+3:wght@400;600&display=swap

#### Signature and product imagery (Guided)
- Embedded product screenshots, app icons and product imagery keep their own colours. They are exempt from the canvas and colour coverage budget and from the accent and primary limits; the studio frame around them (surfaces, captions, borders) follows the brand rules.
- Coverage and whitespace targets apply to the brand's own surfaces, not to product pages that showcase a product's own visual system.

#### Do and don't (Guided)
- Do: Separate everything with hairline rules; use a thick-over-thin double rule once, under the masthead.
- Do: Set captions, tabs and labels in small-caps sans with .12em tracking; keep reading copy in the text serif with a drop cap on the first paragraph.

#### Tokens (CSS) (Locked)
```css
:root {
  color-scheme: light;
  --canvas: #f4ebdb; --ink: #1d1612; --muted: #6e665d;
  --line: #d4cbbd; --primary: #7a2a2a; --on-primary: #ffffff;
  --accent-ink: #7a2a2a; --error: #7a2a2a; --on-error: #ffffff;
  --white: #ffffff; --kit-field: #575048; --primary-text: #7a2a2a;
  --kit-field-2: #c62f2f; --info-icon: #7a2a2a; --ab-hair: rgba(29,22,18,0.3);
  --kit-callout: #898177; --error-icon: #7a2a2a; --kit-badge: #50211f;
  --kit-stepper: #3b3a38; --kit-overlay: rgba(29, 22, 18, 0.34); --ab-hair-soft: rgba(29,22,18,0.16);
  --secondary: #e6dcc6; --on-secondary: #141414; --accent: #7a2a2a;
  --on-accent: #ffffff; --pri-tint: #e5d4c6; --sec-tint: #f2e9d8;
  --font-heading: "Libre Caslon Text", Georgia, serif; --font-body: "Source Sans 3", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --text-display: 34px; --text-eyebrow: 13px; --text-label: 13px; --text-large-title: 34px; --text-title: 28px; --text-title-2: 22px; --text-headline: 17px; --text-body: 17px; --text-callout: 16px; --text-subhead: 15px; --text-footnote: 13px; --text-caption: 12px; --text-tab-label: 10px;
  --space-4: 4px; --space-8: 8px; --space-12: 12px; --space-16: 16px; --space-20: 20px; --space-24: 24px; --space-28: 28px; --space-32: 32px; --space-40: 40px; --space-48: 48px;
  --status-bar: 54px; --home-indicator: 34px; --top-bar: 47px; --top-bar-rule: 1px; --tab-bar: 83px; --margin: 20px;
  --row: 52px; --row-detail: 59px;
  --radius-control: 0px; --radius-field: 0px; --radius-card: 0px; --hit: 44px;
  --shadow-empty: var(--ab-hair) 0 0 0 1px inset; --shadow-avatar: var(--ink) 0 0 0 1px inset;
  --shadow-fab: var(--ink) 0 -3px 0 0 inset; --shadow-listbox: var(--ab-hair) 0 0 0 1px;
  --shadow-matrix: var(--ab-hair) 0 0 0 1px inset; --shadow-context-menu: var(--ab-hair) 0 0 0 1px;
  --shadow-submenu: var(--ab-hair) 0 0 0 1px;
  --dur-fast: 120ms; --dur-base: 180ms; --dur-slow: 280ms; --ease: cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1);
}
:root[data-theme="dark"] {
  color-scheme: dark;
  --canvas: #1d1612; --ink: #f4ecdc; --muted: #9e968b;
  --line: #3d3630; --accent-ink: #f4ecdc; --error: #af7f78;
  --on-error: #1d1612; --kit-field: #b6aea2; --primary-text: #ab7871;
  --kit-field-2: #ff7b72; --info-icon: #995b57; --ab-hair: rgba(244,236,220,0.26);
  --kit-callout: #736c63; --error-icon: #9e625e; --kit-badge: #f4ecdc;
  --kit-overlay: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.38); --ab-hair-soft: rgba(244,236,220,0.14); --pri-tint: #281815;
  --sec-tint: #3d362f;
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { :root { --dur-fast: 0ms; --dur-base: 0ms; --dur-slow: 0ms; } }
```
Follow the system appearance:
```js
const darkMq = matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
const applyTheme = () => { document.documentElement.dataset.theme = darkMq.matches ? 'dark' : 'light'; };
applyTheme(); darkMq.addEventListener('change', applyTheme);
```

#### App icon (Locked)
Full-bleed `#7a2a2a` tile with one simple glyph (or the brand initial in the heading face) in `#ffffff`; flat, no shadow, no gradient. Deliver a 1024x1024 master without transparency or pre-rounded corners (the system masks it), a dark variant on `#1d1612` and a tinted variant (glyph only, one color), and derive every other size from the master. Web: also a 512px maskable icon and a 32px favicon from the same master.

## Part 2: Design tokens

These are the brand's tokens: use them as given, and use nothing else for colour, type or spacing. The first block is the same file "Save + files" writes; the last block holds the values of every custom property the Part 3 CSS reads, resolved on the kit root, so every `var(--x)` in Part 3 has a value here.

### tokens.css

```css
:root {
  color-scheme: light;
  --canvas: #f4ebdb; --ink: #1d1612; --muted: #6e665d;
  --line: #d4cbbd; --primary: #7a2a2a; --on-primary: #ffffff;
  --accent-ink: #7a2a2a; --error: #7a2a2a; --on-error: #ffffff;
  --white: #ffffff; --kit-field: #575048; --primary-text: #7a2a2a;
  --kit-field-2: #c62f2f; --info-icon: #7a2a2a; --ab-hair: rgba(29,22,18,0.3);
  --kit-callout: #898177; --error-icon: #7a2a2a; --kit-badge: #50211f;
  --kit-stepper: #3b3a38; --kit-overlay: rgba(29, 22, 18, 0.34); --ab-hair-soft: rgba(29,22,18,0.16);
  --secondary: #e6dcc6; --on-secondary: #141414; --accent: #7a2a2a;
  --on-accent: #ffffff; --pri-tint: #e5d4c6; --sec-tint: #f2e9d8;
  --font-heading: "Libre Caslon Text", Georgia, serif; --font-body: "Source Sans 3", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --text-display: 34px; --text-eyebrow: 13px; --text-label: 13px; --text-large-title: 34px; --text-title: 28px; --text-title-2: 22px; --text-headline: 17px; --text-body: 17px; --text-callout: 16px; --text-subhead: 15px; --text-footnote: 13px; --text-caption: 12px; --text-tab-label: 10px;
  --space-4: 4px; --space-8: 8px; --space-12: 12px; --space-16: 16px; --space-20: 20px; --space-24: 24px; --space-28: 28px; --space-32: 32px; --space-40: 40px; --space-48: 48px;
  --status-bar: 54px; --home-indicator: 34px; --top-bar: 47px; --top-bar-rule: 1px; --tab-bar: 83px; --margin: 20px;
  --row: 52px; --row-detail: 59px;
  --radius-control: 0px; --radius-field: 0px; --radius-card: 0px; --hit: 44px;
  --shadow-empty: var(--ab-hair) 0 0 0 1px inset; --shadow-avatar: var(--ink) 0 0 0 1px inset;
  --shadow-fab: var(--ink) 0 -3px 0 0 inset; --shadow-listbox: var(--ab-hair) 0 0 0 1px;
  --shadow-matrix: var(--ab-hair) 0 0 0 1px inset; --shadow-context-menu: var(--ab-hair) 0 0 0 1px;
  --shadow-submenu: var(--ab-hair) 0 0 0 1px;
  --dur-fast: 120ms; --dur-base: 180ms; --dur-slow: 280ms; --ease: cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1);
}
:root[data-theme="dark"] {
  color-scheme: dark;
  --canvas: #1d1612; --ink: #f4ecdc; --muted: #9e968b;
  --line: #3d3630; --accent-ink: #f4ecdc; --error: #af7f78;
  --on-error: #1d1612; --kit-field: #b6aea2; --primary-text: #ab7871;
  --kit-field-2: #ff7b72; --info-icon: #995b57; --ab-hair: rgba(244,236,220,0.26);
  --kit-callout: #736c63; --error-icon: #9e625e; --kit-badge: #f4ecdc;
  --kit-overlay: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.38); --ab-hair-soft: rgba(244,236,220,0.14); --pri-tint: #281815;
  --sec-tint: #3d362f;
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { :root { --dur-fast: 0ms; --dur-base: 0ms; --dur-slow: 0ms; } }
```

### Resolved custom properties read by the kit CSS

Set these on the kit root (the element carrying class `ab sb-ab`) before the Part 3 CSS applies. The first block is the light look; the second lists only the values that differ in dark, scoped by `data-mode`.

Set per element (in the markup's inline `style` or by a rule inside `kit.css`, not on the root): `--h`, `--k-picked`, `--pr`, `--t`, `--tone`, `--tone-ico`, `--v`, `--wr`.

Optional hooks this style does not set (each `var()` in `kit.css` carries its own fallback, so leave them unset): `--k-arrow-edge`, `--k-callout-edge-w`, `--k-callout-fill`, `--k-callout-r`, `--k-callout-shadow`, `--k-dis-label`, `--k-sheet-r`, `--k-sheet-shadow`.

## Part 3: UI kit source

First comes `kit.css`: every CSS rule that draws the UI kit in this style, in cascade order, grouped by the file it came from. Then each component, with what it is for, its iOS treatment, its states and the reference markup of every state. Port the CSS values exactly; treat the markup as illustrative.

### Kit root

Every selector in `kit.css` is scoped to this root. Rename or fold the scope into your own, but keep the same attributes' meaning (`data-ae` is the style, `data-mode` is light or dark).

```html
<section class="ab sb-ab sb-window sb-kit" data-ae="editorial-serif" data-mode="light" data-accent-text="ok" data-hero="poster" data-palette="chips" data-sections="asym" data-highlight="custom" data-states="button" data-subcopy="on" data-hcls="old-style" data-bcls="humanist" data-sb-danger="line" data-k-field="line" data-ab-hs0="1" data-ab-hs-fit="1">
  <!-- a component's reference markup goes here -->
</section>
```

### kit.css

639 rules from 4 files: `baseline`, `css/aesthetic-board.css`, `css/aesthetics/editorial-serif.css`, `css/see-built.css`.

```css
/* ===== baseline (from css/styles.css) ===== */
*,
::before,
::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *,
  ::before,
  ::after {
    animation-duration: 0.001ms !important;
    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
    transition-duration: 0.001ms !important;
  }
}

/* ===== css/aesthetic-board.css ===== */
.ab {
  container: ab / inline-size;
  position: relative;
  isolation: isolate;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b));
  --ab-pad: clamp(20px, 4.4cqw, 44px);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 400;
  line-height: 1.5;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  text-transform: none;
  text-align: left;
  border-radius: 20px;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  font-synthesis: none;
}

.ab *,
.ab ::before,
.ab ::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

:where(.ab) button,
:where(.ab) input {
  font: inherit;
  color: inherit;
}

.ab h2,
.ab h3,
.ab p,
.ab ul {
  margin: 0px;
  padding: 0px;
}

.ab i {
  font-style: normal;
}

.ab-brand {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  min-width: 0px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  font-size: clamp(15px, 2cqw, 19px);
}

.ab-wordmark {
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab:not(.ab-slide) .ab-wordmark {
  overflow: visible;
  text-overflow: clip;
  white-space: normal;
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}

.ab-mark {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  font: 700 14px/1 var(--ab-font-h);
}

.ab-rule {
  height: 1px;
  background: var(--ab-line);
}

.ab-label,
.ab-fieldlabel {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label, var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b)));
}

.ab-labelrow {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: baseline;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

.ab-label {
  font-size: 11px;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab-headline {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
  font-size: calc(clamp(32px, 7.4cqw, 70px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  text-wrap: balance;
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

.ab[data-ae] .ab-headline:not([data-wrap-last-resort]) {
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

.ab-hl-glue {
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab-subcopy {
  max-width: 36em;
  text-wrap: pretty;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: clamp(13.5px, 1.5cqw, 16px);
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  margin-top: clamp(14px, 2cqw, 22px) !important;
}

.ab[data-hero="poster"] .ab-headline {
  font-size: calc(clamp(44px, 12.5cqw, 168px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  line-height: 0.9;
  text-wrap: wrap;
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

.ab-card {
  padding: clamp(18px, 2.8cqw, 26px);
  border-radius: 16px;
}

.ab-seg {
  display: flex;
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 5px;
  border-radius: 12px;
}

.ab-seg-btn {
  flex: 1 1 0%;
  min-height: 36px;
  padding: 8px 6px;
  border: 0px;
  background: transparent;
  border-radius: 9px;
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  cursor: pointer;
}

.ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab-card-title {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: clamp(21px, 2.7cqw, 27px);
  line-height: 1.12;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  margin: 24px 0px 8px !important;
}

.ab-card-body {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: 13px;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  margin-bottom: 22px !important;
}

.ab-cta {
  width: 100%;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 13px 16px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  font-size: 13.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab-cta-arrow::before {
  content: "↗";
}

.ab-fieldlabel {
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
  font-size: 12px;
}

.ab .ab-input {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
  padding: 13px 15px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: transparent;
  color: inherit;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: inherit;
  text-transform: none;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  outline: none;
}

.ab[data-ae] .ab-input:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

.ab-toggle {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
  min-height: 44px;
  font-size: 13px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.ab-switch {
  appearance: none;
  position: relative;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  margin: 0px;
  width: 50px;
  height: 29px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: var(--ab-line);
  transition: background 0.18s;
}

.ab-switch::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: 4px;
  top: 4px;
  width: 21px;
  height: 21px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  transition: transform 0.18s;
}

.ab-switch:checked {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab-switch:checked::before {
  transform: translateX(21px);
}

.ab-list {
  list-style: none;
}

.ab-row {
  display: grid;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px clamp(10px, 2cqw, 20px);
  padding: 13px 2px;
  grid-template-columns: 2.6ch minmax(0px, 1fr) auto minmax(60px, 150px) 4.5ch;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  font-size: 13px;
}

.ab-row:last-child {
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
}

.ab-row-bar {
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: var(--ab-line);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.ab-row-bar i {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  background: var(--ab-secondary);
}

.ab-fit {
  display: inline-block;
  padding: 2px 9px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label, var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b)));
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1.5;
  white-space: nowrap;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  text-transform: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab-fit[data-fit="match"] {
  background: var(--ab-sec-tint);
  border-color: transparent;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab-type-apply {
  padding: 10px 16px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab-type-apply:hover {
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
}

.ab-type-apply:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

@container ab (max-width: 600px) {
  .ab-headline {
    font-size: calc(clamp(30px, 10.5cqw, 52px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  }

  .ab[data-hero="poster"] .ab-headline {
    font-size: calc(clamp(40px, 14.6cqw, 72px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  }

  .ab-brand {
    flex: 1 1 auto;
  }

  .ab-labelrow {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    row-gap: 4px;
  }

  .ab-labelrow > .ab-label:last-child:not(:first-child) {
    white-space: nowrap;
  }

  .ab-row {
    grid-template-columns: 2.6ch minmax(0px, 1fr) 4.5ch;
    row-gap: 6px;
  }

  .ab-row-bar {
    display: none;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .ab *,
  .ab ::before,
  .ab ::after {
    transition: none !important;
    animation: auto ease 0s 1 normal none running none !important;
  }
}

/* ===== css/aesthetics/editorial-serif.css ===== */
.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] {
  border-radius: 0px;
  --eds-hl: calc(clamp(44px, 11.5cqw, 160px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  --eds-plate-w: clamp(190px, 29cqw, 370px);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-brand {
  justify-self: center;
  font-weight: 400;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: calc(0.05em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
  font-size: clamp(20px, 3cqw, 34px);
  line-height: 1;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-mark {
  display: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-rule {
  background: var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"][data-hero="poster"] .ab-headline {
  grid-area: 2 / 1;
  font-weight: 400;
  font-size: var(--eds-hl);
  line-height: 0.94;
  letter-spacing: calc(-0.028em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
  text-wrap: balance;
  overflow-wrap: normal;
  hyphens: manual;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-hl {
  font-style: italic;
  font-weight: 400;
  color: var(--ab-accent-ink);
  background: none;
  padding: 0px;
  margin: 0px;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"][data-accent-text="low"] .ab-hl {
  background-image: none;
  color: var(--ab-accent-ink);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-subcopy {
  grid-area: 3 / 1;
  align-self: end;
  padding-top: clamp(12px, 1.8cqw, 20px);
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  font-size: clamp(16px, 2cqw, 23px);
  line-height: 1.38;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  max-width: 30em;
  margin: clamp(22px, 3.4cqw, 42px) 0px 0px !important;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-label {
  font-size: 10.5px;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-labelrow {
  padding-bottom: 9px;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  align-items: baseline;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-fit {
  padding: 2px 8px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  background: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-fit[data-fit="match"] {
  border-color: var(--ab-ink);
  background: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-type-apply {
  padding: 11px 18px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
  border-radius: 0px;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  transition: background 0.15s, color 0.15s;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-type-apply:hover {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-card {
  padding: 0px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-seg {
  gap: 26px;
  padding: 0px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-seg-btn {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  min-height: 0px;
  padding: 0px 0px 10px;
  margin-bottom: -1px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  border-bottom: 2px solid transparent;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  transition: color 0.15s, border-color 0.15s;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-seg-btn:hover {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
  border-bottom-color: var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-card-title {
  font-weight: 400;
  font-size: clamp(28px, 3.6cqw, 42px);
  line-height: 1.02;
  letter-spacing: calc(-0.02em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
  margin: clamp(20px, 3cqw, 32px) 0px 12px !important;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-card-body {
  display: flow-root;
  font-size: 17px;
  line-height: 1.4;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  width: min(100%, 12.5em);
  margin-bottom: 24px !important;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-cta {
  border-radius: 0px;
  padding: 15px 18px;
  font-size: 11.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.18em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  box-shadow: none;
  transition: background 0.15s, color 0.15s, transform 0.1s;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-cta:hover {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-cta:active {
  background: var(--ab-eds-primary-press);
  color: var(--ab-eds-on-press);
  transform: translateY(1px);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-cta-arrow::before {
  content: "→";
  font-size: 15px;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-fieldlabel {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.18em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  margin-bottom: 4px;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-input {
  padding: 8px 0px 9px;
  border-top: 0px;
  border-right: 0px;
  border-left: 0px;
  border-image: none;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-hair-strong);
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 24px;
  line-height: 1.2;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  transition: border-color 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-input:focus {
  border-bottom-color: var(--ab-primary);
  box-shadow: 0 1px 0 var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-toggle {
  padding: 10px 0px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-hair-soft);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-hair-soft);
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-switch {
  width: 40px;
  height: 18px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-hair-strong);
  transition: background 0.15s;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-switch::before {
  left: 3px;
  top: 3px;
  width: 12px;
  height: 12px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  transition: transform 0.15s, background 0.15s;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-switch:checked {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-switch:checked::before {
  transform: translateX(22px);
  background: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-list {
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-hair-strong);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-row {
  grid-template-columns: 3.4ch auto minmax(20px, 1fr) auto 5ch;
  align-items: baseline;
  gap: 0px clamp(8px, 1.4cqw, 14px);
  padding: clamp(11px, 1.5cqw, 16px) 0px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-hair-soft);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-row:first-child {
  border-top: 0px;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-row:last-child {
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-row-bar {
  grid-area: 1 / 3;
  display: block;
  align-self: end;
  height: 0px;
  margin-bottom: 0.3em;
  border-bottom: 1px dotted var(--ab-hair-strong);
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-row-bar i {
  display: none;
}

.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-seg-btn:focus-visible,
.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-cta:focus-visible,
.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-switch:focus-visible,
.ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-type-apply:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}

@container ab (max-width: 600px) {
  .ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-brand {
    font-size: 16px;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"][data-hero="poster"] .ab-headline {
    font-size: calc(clamp(44px, 17cqw, 78px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  }

  .ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-subcopy {
    border-top: 0px;
    padding-top: 0px;
    font-size: 16px;
    margin-top: 18px !important;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-row {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0px, auto) minmax(14px, 1fr) auto auto;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-row-bar {
    display: block;
    grid-column: 2;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-cta:active {
    transform: none;
  }
}

@container ab (max-width: 360px) {
  .ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-row {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0px, 1fr) auto auto;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"] .ab-row-bar {
    display: none;
  }
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit {
  --k-r: 0px;
  --ab-primary-text: var(--ab-ink) !important;
  --ab-pri-tint: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 6%, var(--ab-canvas)) !important;
  --ab-accent: var(--ab-ink) !important;
  --ab-on-accent: var(--ab-canvas) !important;
  --ab-secondary: var(--ab-canvas) !important;
  --ab-on-secondary: var(--ab-ink) !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit :is(.ab-cta.sb-k-btn, .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2) {
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px) !important;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2 {
  font-weight: 500;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-btn.sb-k-pressed {
  background: var(--ab-eds-primary-press);
  color: var(--ab-eds-on-press);
  filter: none;
  transform: translateY(1px);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-pressed {
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
  filter: none;
  transform: translateY(1px);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-textbtn {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-danger {
  box-shadow: none;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit :is(.sb-k-field, .sb-k-search).is-focus .ab-input {
  box-shadow: 0 1px 0 var(--ab-ink) !important;
  border-color: var(--ab-ink) !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-field.is-error .ab-input {
  box-shadow: 0 1px 0 var(--sb-danger) !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-search .ab-input {
  padding-left: 30px !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-search > .sb-ico {
  left: 0px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-menu {
  border-color: var(--ab-hair-strong);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-box:checked {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  border-color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-box[type="checkbox"]:checked::after {
  border-color: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-box[type="radio"]:checked::after {
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-toggle.sb-tog {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-weight: 400;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  text-transform: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-switch:checked {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-switch:checked::before {
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-seg-btn {
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px) !important;
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px) !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  border-bottom-color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"] {
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"]::after {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-card:is(.sb-k-card, .sb-home-card) {
  padding-left: 0px !important;
  padding-right: 0px !important;
  padding-bottom: 0px !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-card .ab-card-body {
  width: auto;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-labelrow.sb-k-section {
  padding-bottom: 0px;
  border-bottom: 0px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit :is(.sb-k-ico, .sb-k-emptyico) {
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-av {
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-hair-strong);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 400;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-avs .sb-k-av {
  margin-left: -4px;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-hair-strong), 0 0 0 2px var(--ab-canvas);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-callout {
  padding: 14px 0px;
  border-right: 0px;
  border-left: 0px;
  border-image: none;
  border-top: 2px solid var(--tone-ico, var(--tone));
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  background: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-callout[data-tone="info"] {
  --tone: var(--ab-ink);
  --tone-ico: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit :is(.sb-k-badge, .sb-k-count) {
  border-radius: 0px;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-badge {
  background: none;
  color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-accent-ink) 55%, var(--ab-ink));
  box-shadow: currentcolor 0px 0px 0px 1px inset;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-count:not(.is-accent) {
  background: none;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-badge {
  border-radius: 0px;
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-row-bar.sb-k-bar {
  position: relative;
  margin: 0px;
  border: 0px;
  background: var(--ab-hair);
  align-self: auto;
  height: 2px !important;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-row-bar.sb-k-bar i {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-range::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
  height: 2px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ab-ink) var(--v, 50%), var(--ab-hair) var(--v, 50%));
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-range::-webkit-slider-thumb {
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  margin-top: -8px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-tab[aria-current]::before {
  border-radius: 0px;
  height: 2px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-toast {
  border-radius: 0px;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-sheet {
  border-radius: 0px;
  box-shadow: none;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-hair-strong);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-fit {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  min-height: 22px;
  padding: 0px 9px;
  font-size: var(--k-caption, 12px);
  line-height: var(--k-caption-lh, 16px);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-ph {
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 6%, var(--ab-canvas));
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-lightbox .sb-k-lb-img.sb-k-ph {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-canvas) 14%, var(--ab-ink));
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-canvas) 45%, transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit:not([data-mode="dark"]) :is(.sb-k-dialogbox, .sb-k-sidebox, .sb-k-drawerbox)::before,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit:not([data-mode="dark"]) .sb-k-scrim {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 34%, transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit :is(.sb-k-cmdmenu, .sb-k-listbox, .sb-k-combo .sb-k-menu) li {
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit :is(.sb-k-cmdmenu, .sb-k-listbox, .sb-k-combo .sb-k-menu) li:not(.sb-k-menu-sep) + li:not(.sb-k-menu-sep) {
  border-top: 1px dotted var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit :is(.sb-k-cmdmenu, .sb-k-listbox, .sb-k-combo .sb-k-menu) li[aria-selected="true"] {
  box-shadow: inset 2px 0 0 var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.sb-k-menu-sep {
  background: var(--ab-hair-strong);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.is-open {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.is-open .sb-ico {
  color: inherit;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-pop-arrow {
  border-color: var(--ab-hair-strong);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-split > .sb-k-split-more {
  border-left-color: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-fab {
  border-radius: 0px;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 3px var(--ab-primary), inset 0 0 0 4px color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-on-primary) 55%, transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-fabbox {
  border-color: var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-link {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  text-decoration-thickness: 1px;
  text-underline-offset: 3px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-link.sb-k-pressed {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
  font-style: italic;
  text-decoration-thickness: 2px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-link.sb-k-focus {
  text-decoration-thickness: 2px;
  outline-offset: 3px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-icon {
  box-sizing: border-box;
  padding: 10px 12px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-hair);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-icon > span {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-match {
  font-style: italic;
  font-weight: 600;
  box-shadow: inset 0 -1px 0 var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-step-l {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px);
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-step-val {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 400;
  font-size: 22px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-knob-arc {
  background: conic-gradient(from -135deg, var(--ab-ink) calc(var(--v) * 270deg), var(--ab-hair) 0deg 270deg, transparent 0deg);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-knob-dial {
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-knob-ind {
  width: 2px;
  margin-left: -1px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-knob-txt b {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-knob-val {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 20px;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-date-ico {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-cal {
  border-right: 0px;
  border-left: 0px;
  border-image: none;
  border-top: 3px solid var(--ab-hair-strong);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-hair-strong);
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-cal-t {
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-cal-dow {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  font-weight: 500;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-day {
  border-radius: 0px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-size: 17px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-day.is-today {
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-day.is-range {
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
  box-shadow: inset 0 1px 0 var(--ab-hair), inset 0 -1px 0 var(--ab-hair);
}

}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-trail {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-acc {
  border-top: 0px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-hair-strong);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-acc-sum .sb-k-title {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 400;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-acc[open] > .sb-k-acc-sum {
  box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-divider .ab-rule {
  height: 5px;
  background: linear-gradient(var(--ab-hair-strong), var(--ab-hair-strong)) 0 0 / 100% 3px no-repeat, linear-gradient(var(--ab-hair-strong), var(--ab-hair-strong)) 0 100% / 100% 1px no-repeat;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-section .ab-label,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-mx-label .ab-label {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.18em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-callout b {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-style: italic;
  font-weight: 400;
  font-size: 18px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-card.sb-k-card.sb-k-dialog {
  border: 3px double var(--ab-hair-strong);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .ab-card.sb-k-card.sb-k-pop {
  border: 3px double var(--ab-hair-strong);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-dialog-t,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-sheet-t,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-pop .ab-card-title,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-tip-t {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-style: italic;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-sheet {
  border-top: 3px double var(--ab-hair-strong);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-grab {
  height: 3px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  width: 44px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-pop-arrow {
  border-width: 3px 0px 0px 3px;
  border-style: double;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-pop-arrow.is-down {
  border-width: 0px 3px 3px 0px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-tooltip {
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-topbar {
  border-top: 3px solid var(--ab-ink);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-tb-title {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 400;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabbarbox {
  border-right: 0px;
  border-left: 0px;
  border-image: none;
  border-top: 3px solid var(--ab-ink);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
  border-radius: 0px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-tab {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  font-size: 10px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-tab[aria-current] {
  font-weight: 700;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs {
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"] {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
  text-transform: uppercase;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"] {
  font-weight: 700;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-tabs [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"]::after {
  height: 3px;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-nm-item.ab-row.sb-k-row[aria-current="page"] {
  box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-toast {
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  border-right: 0px;
  border-left: 0px;
  border-image: none;
  border-top: 3px solid var(--ab-ink);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-toast button {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
  font-style: normal;
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-empty-t {
  font-style: italic;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-badge,
.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-count {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-count {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="editorial-serif"].sb-kit .sb-k-spinner {
  padding-left: 8px;
}

/* ===== css/see-built.css ===== */
.sb-single > .sb-window {
  min-height: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) {
  --sb-danger: #c62f2f;
  --sb-danger-line: rgba(198, 47, 47, 0.35);
  --sb-danger-on: #fff;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) {
  --sb-danger-text: color-mix(in srgb, var(--sb-danger) 62%, var(--ab-ink, #111));
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen)[data-mode="dark"] {
  --sb-danger: #ff7b72;
  --sb-danger-line: rgba(255, 123, 114, 0.4);
  --sb-danger-on: #1a0505;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .sb-ico {
  display: block;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) :is(button, a) {
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}

.ab.sb-ab.sb-window {
  min-height: 100%;
}

.sb-search {
  position: relative;
  width: clamp(180px, 24cqw, 260px);
}

.sb-search > .sb-ico {
  position: absolute;
  left: 12px;
  top: 50%;
  translate: 0px -50%;
  z-index: 1;
  color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 28%, var(--ab-muted));
  pointer-events: none;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .sb-search .ab-input {
  margin: 0px;
  padding-top: 10px;
  padding-bottom: 10px;
  padding-left: 36px;
  font-size: 13px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .ab-type-apply.sb-icon-btn {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 42px;
  height: 42px;
  padding: 0px;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
}

.sb-badge {
  position: absolute;
  top: 8px;
  right: 9px;
  width: 8px;
  height: 8px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-accent);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px var(--ab-canvas);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .ab-row[tabindex] {
  cursor: pointer;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .ab-row[tabindex]:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: -2px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .ab-row.is-picked {
  background-color: var(--ab-pri-tint);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .sb-field .ab-input {
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .ab-toggle.sb-tog {
  min-height: 54px;
  padding: 6px 0px;
}

.sb-phone-in {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  height: 100%;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  padding: 54px var(--k-margin, 20px) 0;
}

.sb-phone-in > * {
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.sb-statusbar {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0px;
  left: 0px;
  right: 0px;
  height: 54px;
  padding: 4px 30px 0px 46px;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  font: 600 17px / 1 -apple-system, system-ui, sans-serif;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  text-transform: none;
}

.sb-sb-r {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
}

.sb-sig {
  width: 18px;
  height: 11px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, currentcolor 0px, currentcolor 3px, transparent 3px, transparent 5px, currentcolor 5px, currentcolor 8px, transparent 8px, transparent 10px, currentcolor 10px, currentcolor 13px, transparent 13px, transparent 15px, currentcolor 15px, currentcolor 18px);
  clip-path: polygon(0px 75%, 100% 0px, 100% 100%, 0px 100%);
}

.sb-wifi {
  width: 16px;
  height: 11px;
  border-radius: 14px 14px 0px 0px;
  background: currentcolor;
  clip-path: polygon(50% 100%, 0px 20%, 100% 20%);
}

.sb-bat {
  position: relative;
  width: 26px;
  height: 13px;
  border: 1.5px solid;
  border-radius: 4px;
  opacity: 0.9;
}

.sb-bat::after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 1.5px 6px 1.5px 1.5px;
  border-radius: 1.5px;
  background: currentcolor;
}

.sb-homebar {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 3;
  bottom: 8px;
  left: 50%;
  width: 134px;
  height: 5px;
  margin-left: -67px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  opacity: 0.85;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-headline.sb-ob-h {
  max-width: none;
  text-wrap: balance;
  margin: 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-large-title, 34px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-large-title-lh, 41px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-subcopy.sb-ob-body {
  max-width: none;
  margin: 12px 0px 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-body-lh, 24px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) .sb-k-row.sb-k-cut {
  display: none !important;
}

.sb-tabs {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 2;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0px, 1fr));
  align-items: start;
  height: var(--k-tab, 83px);
  margin: auto calc(-1 * var(--k-margin, 20px)) 0;
  padding: 6px 6px 34px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-canvas) 92%, transparent);
}

.sb-tab {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  justify-items: center;
  gap: 3px;
  min-width: 0px;
  padding: 2px 0px 6px;
  border: 0px;
  background: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-family: var(--sb-ui-ff, var(--ab-font-ui));
  font-size: var(--k-tab-label, 10px);
  line-height: var(--k-tab-label-lh, 12px);
  font-weight: 500;
  color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 28%, var(--ab-muted));
  text-transform: var(--sb-ui-tt, none);
  letter-spacing: var(--sb-ui-ls, 0);
}

.sb-tab span {
  max-width: 100%;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.sb-tab[aria-current] {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.sb-tab[aria-current]::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 0px;
  left: 50%;
  width: 16px;
  margin-left: -8px;
  height: 3px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

.sb-tab[aria-current] > .sb-ico {
  color: var(--k-pri-text, var(--ab-primary-text, var(--ab-primary)));
}

.sb-tab:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: -2px;
}

.sb-k-mid {
  margin: 0px 0.12em;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.ab-card, .ab-panel, .ab-list) {
  --k-muted: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 28%, var(--ab-muted));
  --k-picked: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-primary) 12%, transparent);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-row.sb-k-row.is-picked {
  background-color: var(--k-picked, var(--ab-pri-tint));
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit {
  --k-ui: var(--sb-ui-ff, var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b)));
  --k-muted: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 28%, var(--ab-muted));
  --k-r: min(var(--sb-r, 12px), 16px);
  --sb-focus-w: 2px;
  --sb-focus-offset: 2px;
}

:where(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-focus,
:where(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :where(.sb-k-btn, .sb-k-btn2, .sb-k-textbtn, .sb-k-danger, .sb-k-iconbtn):focus-visible {
  outline: var(--sb-focus-w) solid var(--sb-focus, var(--sb-focus-auto, var(--ab-ink)));
  outline-offset: var(--sb-focus-offset);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.ab-cta.sb-k-btn, .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2) {
  width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  white-space: normal;
  min-height: var(--k-btn, 52px) !important;
  padding-top: 0px !important;
  padding-bottom: 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-headline, 17px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-headline-lh, 22px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2 {
  justify-content: center;
  text-align: center;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-btn .ab-cta-arrow {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2.sb-k-auto {
  width: auto;
  padding-left: 20px !important;
  padding-right: 20px !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-pressed {
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 100vmax color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 7%, transparent);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-btn, .sb-k-btn2, .sb-k-textbtn, .sb-k-danger, .sb-k-iconbtn):disabled {
  cursor: not-allowed;
  color: var(--k-dis-label, var(--k-muted)) !important;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 100vmax color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-canvas) 58%, transparent) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-btn, .sb-k-btn2):disabled * {
  color: inherit !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-box, .ab-switch):disabled {
  opacity: 0.5;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-check, .ab-toggle).is-disabled {
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-check, .ab-toggle).is-disabled > span {
  color: var(--k-muted) !important;
}

.sb-k-textbtn {
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  padding: 0px 4px;
  border: 0px;
  background: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-family: var(--k-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-headline, 17px);
  line-height: var(--k-headline-lh, 22px);
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--k-pri-text, var(--ab-primary-text, var(--ab-ink)));
  text-transform: var(--sb-ui-tt, none);
  letter-spacing: var(--sb-ui-ls, 0);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-textbtn.sb-k-pressed {
  box-shadow: none;
  color: var(--k-muted);
}

.sb-k-danger {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: var(--k-btn, 52px);
  padding: 0px 20px;
  cursor: pointer;
  border: 1px solid var(--sb-danger);
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-r, 10px), 999px);
  background: var(--sb-danger);
  color: var(--sb-danger-on, #fff);
  font-family: var(--k-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-headline, 17px);
  font-weight: 600;
  text-transform: var(--sb-ui-tt, none);
  letter-spacing: var(--sb-ui-ls, 0);
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04), 0 6px 16px -8px color-mix(in srgb, var(--sb-danger) 35%, transparent);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen)[data-sb-danger="line"] .sb-k-danger {
  border: 1.5px solid var(--sb-danger);
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--sb-danger-text, var(--sb-danger));
  box-shadow: none;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen)[data-sb-danger="line"].sb-kit .sb-k-danger.sb-k-pressed {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--sb-danger) 10%, transparent);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-type-apply.sb-k-iconbtn {
  box-sizing: border-box;
  width: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  min-width: var(--k-tap, 44px) !important;
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px) !important;
  padding: 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-plain {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  padding: 0px;
  border: 0px;
  background: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  cursor: pointer;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-split {
  display: flex;
  width: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-split > .sb-k-split-main {
  flex: 1 1 0px;
  width: auto;
  min-width: 0px;
  border-top-right-radius: 0px !important;
  border-bottom-right-radius: 0px !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-split > .sb-k-split-more {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  width: var(--k-btn, 52px);
  margin-left: -1px;
  border-left: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-on-primary) 40%, transparent);
  cursor: pointer;
  min-height: var(--k-btn, 52px) !important;
  padding: 0px !important;
  border-top-left-radius: 0px !important;
  border-bottom-left-radius: 0px !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-splitwrap > .sb-k-menu {
  width: min(100%, 240px);
  margin: 6px 0px 0px auto !important;
}

.sb-k-fabbox {
  position: relative;
  width: 100%;
  padding-bottom: 76px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-fabbox > :is(.sb-k-fab, .sb-k-backtop) {
  position: absolute;
  right: 16px;
  bottom: 16px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-cta.sb-k-fab {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 56px;
  height: 56px;
  cursor: pointer;
  min-width: 0px !important;
  min-height: 0px !important;
  padding: 0px !important;
}

:where(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-fab, .sb-k-split-more):focus-visible {
  outline: var(--sb-focus-w, 2px) solid var(--sb-focus, var(--sb-focus-auto, var(--ab-ink)));
  outline-offset: var(--sb-focus-offset, 2px);
}

.sb-k-link {
  color: var(--k-pri-text, var(--ab-primary-text, var(--ab-ink)));
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-underline-offset: 3px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-link.sb-k-pressed {
  box-shadow: none;
  color: var(--k-muted);
}

.sb-k-link:focus-visible {
  outline: var(--sb-focus-w, 2px) solid var(--sb-focus, var(--sb-focus-auto, var(--ab-ink)));
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-field .ab-fieldlabel,
:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-selectwrap .ab-fieldlabel {
  margin: 0px 0px 8px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-footnote-lh, 18px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit :is(.sb-k-field, .sb-k-search, .sb-k-selectwrap) .ab-input {
  min-height: 0px;
  line-height: normal;
  height: var(--k-field, 48px) !important;
  margin: 0px !important;
  padding-top: 0px !important;
  padding-bottom: 0px !important;
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-field.is-focus .ab-input,
:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-search.is-focus .ab-input {
  border-color: var(--k-pri-text, var(--ab-primary-text)) !important;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-primary) 24%, transparent) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-field.is-error .ab-input {
  border-color: var(--sb-danger) !important;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--sb-danger) 18%, transparent) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen)[data-k-field="line"].sb-kit :is(.sb-k-field, .sb-k-search).is-focus .ab-input {
  box-shadow: inset 0 -1px 0 var(--k-pri-text, var(--ab-primary-text)) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen)[data-k-field="line"].sb-kit .sb-k-field.is-error .ab-input {
  box-shadow: inset 0 -1px 0 var(--sb-danger) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-field .ab-input:disabled {
  opacity: 0.5;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

.sb-k-help {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: var(--k-footnote, 13px);
  line-height: var(--k-footnote-lh, 18px);
  color: var(--k-muted);
  margin: 6px 0px 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-help.is-error {
  color: var(--sb-danger-text, var(--sb-danger));
}

.sb-k-search {
  width: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .sb-k-search .ab-input {
  padding-left: 44px !important;
}

.sb-k-search > .sb-ico {
  left: 14px;
}

.sb-k-selectwrap {
  width: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-input.sb-k-select {
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  width: 100%;
  text-align: left;
  cursor: pointer;
  display: flex !important;
}

.sb-k-menu {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 6px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: var(--k-r);
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04) 0px 1px 2px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16) 0px 10px 24px -10px;
  margin: 6px 0px 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-menu li {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  padding: 0px 12px;
  border-radius: max(calc(var(--k-r) - 4px), 0px);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px);
}

.sb-k-menu li[aria-selected="true"] {
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.sb-k-menu .sb-ico {
  color: var(--k-pri-on-tint, var(--ab-primary-text));
}

.sb-k-check {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px);
  line-height: var(--k-body-lh, 24px);
  cursor: pointer;
}

.sb-k-box {
  appearance: none;
  position: relative;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 24px;
  height: 24px;
  margin: 0px;
  cursor: pointer;
  border: 1.5px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 72%, transparent);
  border-radius: min(var(--sb-r, 6px), 7px);
  background: transparent;
}

.sb-k-box[type="radio"] {
  border-radius: 50%;
}

.sb-k-box:checked {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  border-color: var(--ab-primary);
}

.sb-k-box[type="checkbox"]:checked::after {
  content: "";
  width: 12px;
  height: 6px;
  border-left: 2.5px solid var(--ab-on-primary);
  border-bottom: 2.5px solid var(--ab-on-primary);
  transform: translateY(-2px) rotate(-45deg);
}

.sb-k-box[type="radio"]:checked::after {
  content: "";
  width: 10px;
  height: 10px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.sb-k-box:focus-visible {
  outline: var(--sb-focus-w, 2px) solid var(--sb-focus, var(--sb-focus-auto, var(--ab-ink)));
  outline-offset: var(--sb-focus-offset, 2px);
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-toggle.sb-tog {
  width: 100%;
  line-height: var(--k-body-lh, 24px);
  font-size: var(--k-body, 17px) !important;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-seg {
  width: 100%;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen).sb-kit .ab-seg-btn {
  white-space: nowrap;
  min-height: var(--k-tap, 44px) !important;
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px) !important;
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px) !important;
}

.sb-k-slider,
.sb-k-progress {
  width: 100%;
}

.sb-k-slider-h {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-subhead, 15px);
  line-height: var(--k-subhead-lh, 20px);
}

.sb-k-slider-h span:last-child {
  color: var(--k-muted);
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) input[type="range"].sb-k-range {
  appearance: none;
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 28px;
  margin: 0px;
  background: transparent;
  cursor: pointer;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) input[type="range"].sb-k-range::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
  height: 4px;
  border-radius: 2px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ab-primary) var(--v, 50%), color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 16%, transparent) var(--v, 50%));
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) input[type="range"].sb-k-range::-webkit-slider-thumb {
  appearance: none;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  margin-top: -12px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 14%, transparent);
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06) 0px 1px 2px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16) 0px 3px 8px -2px;
  transform: none;
}

:is(.ab.sb-ab, .sb-gen) input[type="range"].sb-k-range:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 4px;
}

.sb-k-icons {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: flex-end;
  gap: 20px;
}

.sb-k-icon {
  display: grid;
  justify-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui);
  font-size: var(--k-caption, 12px);
  line-height: var(--k-caption-lh, 16px);
}

.sb-k-icon > span {
  color: var(--k-muted);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.sb-k-combo {
  width: 100%;
}

.sb-k-match {
  background: none;
  color: inherit;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.sb-k-listbox {
  width: 100%;
  box-shadow: none;
  margin: 0px !important;
}

.sb-k-listbox li > span {
  flex: 1 1 0%;
  min-width: 0px;
```
